# saveupdater
!! This file is for updating save game when one variable have been replaced by another.
!! The version number is updated for releases and when it is required because we cannot run checks to block repeated updating

!! The version number is in saveg.gsrc should only be updated by the person preparing the release, if it needs to be done outside of releases then inform the person in charge of releases to ensure correct number change is made.

!! When updating the version number: at the bottom of this file just after the last end add the following line:
!! if ((100*version_major +version_minor)*100+version_revision)*100+version_patch  < "new version number":
!! where "new version number" should either match that in saveg.qsrc or be for the next minor increment
!! Then add any replacement variables after this line. You can see how this is done by looking through this file.

!! When changing version number you must commit this file and saveg.qsrc at the same time.


temp_current_save_version = ((100 * version_major + version_minor) * 100 + version_revision) * 100 + version_patch

		!!                       VERSION 0.8.4                        !!

	i += 1
	if i < arrsize('lover'): jump 'girlfriend_looop'
	killvar 'i'

	if analorgasm > 0: orgasm_anal += analorgasm
end

if temp_current_save_version < 00080804:

	!! --------------------------------------------------------------------
	!! Homes, Properties and Internet Update
	!! --------------------------------------------------------------------

	!! Transferring the owned, rented, accessible properties
	!! city apartment
	if home_owned[1] = 1 or owned_property['city_apartment'] = 1:
		gs 'homes_properties', 'rent_property', 'city_apartment', ArendHouseSL

	elseif home_owned[1] = 2 or owned_property['city_apartment'] = 2:
		gs 'homes_properties', 'buy_property', 'city_apartment'

	elseif home_owned[1] = 3 or owned_property['city_apartment'] = 3:
		gs 'homes_properties', 'tenants_move_in', 'city_apartment', sdaday, sdamonth
	end

	!! Checking for renovation
	if home_owned[1] > 0 or owned_property['city_apartment'] = 1:
		if rembedr = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'renovate_property', 'city_apartment', 50000, 'bedroom'
		if remsitr = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'renovate_property', 'city_apartment', 50000, 'living room'
		if remkorr = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'renovate_property', 'city_apartment', 50000, 'hallway'
		if remvanr = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'renovate_property', 'city_apartment', 50000, 'bathroom'
		if remkuhr = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'renovate_property', 'city_apartment', 50000, 'kitchen'
	end

	!! parents home
	if home_owned[2]  > 0 or owned_property['parents_home']              > 0: gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access',       'parents_home'

	!! cottage in the village
	if home_owned[3]  > 0 or owned_property['village_cottage']           > 0: gs 'homes_properties', 'buy_property',      'village_cottage'
	if home_owned[3]  > 1 or owned_property['village_cottage-renovated'] = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'renovate_property', 'village_cottage', 200000

	!! Pushking old town apartment
	if home_owned[4]  = 1 or owned_property['old_town_apartment']        = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'rent_property',     'old_town_apartment', ArendHouseSL4

	!! Matryona mansion
	if home_owned[5]  > 0 or owned_property['matryona_mansion']                     > 0: gs 'homes_properties', 'buy_property',                 'matryona_mansion'
	if home_owned[5]  > 1 or owned_property['matryona_mansion-construction-status'] = 1: gs 'homes_properties', 'progress_construction_status', 'matryona_mansion'
	if home_owned[5]  > 2 or owned_property['matryona_mansion-construction-status'] = 2: gs 'homes_properties', 'progress_construction_status', 'matryona_mansion'

	!! Pavlovsk hotel room
	if home_owned[6]  > 0 or owned_property['pavlovsk_hotel']     > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'pavlovsk_hotel'

	!! Live in maid bedroom
	if home_owned[7]  > 0 or owned_property['maid_bedroom']       > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'maid_bedroom'

	!! Niko apartment
	if home_owned[8]  > 0 or owned_property['niko_apartment']     > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'niko_apartment'

	!! Shulga apartment
	if home_owned[9]  > 0 or owned_property['shulga_apartment']   > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'shulga_apartment'

	!! Lyceum dorm room
	if home_owned[10] > 0 or owned_property['lyceum_dorm']        > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'lyceum_dorm'

	!! University dorm room
	if home_owned[11] > 0 or owned_property['university_dorm']    > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'university_dorm'

	!! Hunters lodge
	if home_owned[12] > 0 or owned_property['hunters_lodge']      > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'hunters_lodge'

	!! Shared apartment in Pavlovsk
	if home_owned[13] > 0 or owned_property['shared_apartment']   > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'shared_apartment'

	!! Grandparents house
	if home_owned[14] > 0 or owned_property['grandparents_house'] > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'grandparents_house'

	!! City residential house
	if home_owned[15] > 0 or owned_property['city_house']         > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'buy_property', 'city_house'

	!! Meynold household
	if home_owned[16] > 0 or owned_property['meynold_household']  > 0:  gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'meynold_household'



	!! Setting the current home and fixing potential missing access issues
	if curr_home > 0 and $home['current'] = '':
		gs 'homes_properties', 'set_home', '', str(curr_home)
	end
	if $home['current'] = 'parents_home' and accessible_property['parents_home'] = 0:
		gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'parents_home'
	end

	killvar 'home_owned'
	killvar 'curr_home'
	killvar 'ArendHouseSL' & killvar 'ArendHouseSL4'
	killvar 'home_rent_txt'
	killvar 'home_value'   & killvar 'home_value_txt'
	killvar 'sdaday'       & killvar 'sdamonth'
	killvar 'owned_property'


	if $home['current'] = '':
		if mid($start_type, 1, 2) = 'sg':
			gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'parents_home'
			gs 'homes_properties', 'set_home',    'parents_home'

		elseif mid($start_type, 1, 4) = 'city':
			gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'city_apartment'
			gs 'homes_properties', 'set_home',    'city_apartment'

		elseif mid($start_type, 1, 3) = 'uni':
			gs 'homes_properties', 'give_access', 'university_dorm'
			gs 'homes_properties', 'set_home',    'university_dorm'
		end
	end


	!! Setting the internet
	if internet > 0:
		old_internet_value = 10 * internet
		if property['internet_enabled'] = 1:
			gs 'internet_mobile', 'buy_subscription', $home['current'], 900

			if (old_internet_value - 900) >= 0: money += (old_internet_value - 900)
		else
			if old_internet_value >= 1200:
				gs 'internet_mobile', 'buy_subscription', 'mobile', 1200

				if (old_internet_value - 1200) > 0: money += (old_internet_value - 1200)
			else
				gs 'internet_mobile', 'top_up_metered', 'metered_mobile', internet * 60
			end
		end
		killvar 'internet'
	end

	!! Setting the mobile phone calls and texts
	if balans > 0:
		if (balans - 800) >= 0:
			gs 'internet_mobile', 'buy_subscription', 'monthly_calls', 800
			money += (balans - 800)
		else
			if balans >= 225:
				temp_phone_mult = balans / 225
				temp_phone_rem  = balans mod 225
				new_balans      = temp_phone_mult * 240

				gs 'internet_mobile', 'top_up_metered', 'metered_calls', new_balans

				if temp_phone_rem > 0: money += temp_phone_rem

			elseif balans >= 170:
				temp_phone_rem = balans mod 170
				gs 'internet_mobile', 'top_up_metered', 'metered_calls', 180
				if temp_phone_rem > 0: money += temp_phone_rem

			elseif balans >= 115:
				temp_phone_rem = balans mod 115
				gs 'internet_mobile', 'top_up_metered', 'metered_calls', 120
				if temp_phone_rem > 0: money += temp_phone_rem

			elseif balans >= 60:
				temp_phone_rem = balans mod 60
				gs 'internet_mobile', 'top_up_metered', 'metered_calls', 60
				if temp_phone_rem > 0: money += temp_phone_rem
			else
				gs 'internet_mobile', 'top_up_metered', 'metered_calls', balans
			end
		end

		killvar 'balans' & killvar 'temp_phone_rem' & killvar 'temp_phone_mult' & killvar 'new_balans'
	end


	!! ------------ NPC Stat Updater ------------------------

	if $ARGS[0] = 'npc_stat_update':
		if npc_start_free_time[$boy] = 0 or npc_day_off[$boy] = 0:
			if npc_finance[$boy] = 0:
				if rand(1, 3) = 1:
					npc_start_free_time[$boy] = 9
					npc_end_free_time[$boy] = 12

				elseif rand(1, 2) = 1:
					npc_start_free_time[$boy] = 16
					npc_end_free_time[$boy] = 20
				else
					npc_start_free_time[$boy] = 20
					npc_end_free_time[$boy] = 23
				end

			elseif npc_finance[$boy] = 1:
				if rand(1, 2) = 1:
					npc_start_free_time[$boy] = 9
					npc_end_free_time[$boy] = 12
				else
					npc_start_free_time[$boy] = 20
					npc_end_free_time[$boy] = 23
				end

			elseif npc_finance[$npclastgenerated] = 2:
				npc_apt_type[$npclastgenerated] = 5
				!! npc_apt_type[$npclastgenerated] = rand(5,6)
				!! apt_type 6 is disabled until pictures are added
				npc_occupation[$npclastgenerated] = rand(14, 18)
				npc_car[$npclastgenerated] = rand(0, 1)
			end

			npc_day_off = rand(1, 7)
		end
	end
end

if temp_current_save_version < 00090203:
	!! updates the exp to the new scale so you don''t go down in stat
	gs 'stat_sklattrib'

	x = 1
	:rstloop_outer
		i = 0
		:rstloop_inner
			expadj = 20 * difficulty
			tl = 0
			:trtrstloop
				tltp = 0
				:trtrstinsdlp
					!! Note this loop is done with a flag set in the dynamic that is checked outside; this is because testing showed inconsistent results when jumping from inside the dynamic and with the whole loop in the dynamic.
					dynamic "
						if <<$traitattskl[tl]>> > 0 and <<$traitattskl[tl]>>[tltp + 1] = x and <<$traitattskl[tl]>>[tltp + 2] = i and <<$traitattskl[tl]>>[tltp + 3] <> 0: expadj += <<$traitattskl[tl]>>[tltp + 3]
						if <<$traitattskl[tl]>>[tltp + 11] <> 0: insdlpflag = 1
						"
				if insdlpflag = 1:
					insdlpflag = 0
					tltp += 10
					jump 'trtrstinsdlp'
				end
				tl += 1
			if tl < arrsize ('$traitattskl'): jump 'trtrstloop'
			killvar 'tl' & killvar 'tltp' & killvar 'insdlpflag'

			if x = 1:
				$attskltmp = $att_name[i]
			else
				$attskltmp = $skl_name[i]
			end

			dynamic "
				if <<$attskltmp>>_lvl > 0:
					<<$attskltmp>>_exp -= (expadj * (<<$attskltmp>>_lvl - 1) * (<<$attskltmp>>_lvl - 1) / 180)
					<<$attskltmp>>_exp += (expadj *73* (<<$attskltmp>>_lvl - 1) * (<<$attskltmp>>_lvl - 1) / 2730)
					<<$attskltmp>>_mem = <<$attskltmp>>_exp
				end
			"
			killvar 'expadj'
			killvar 'attskltmp'
			i += 1
		if (x = 1 and i < arrsize ('$att_name')) or (x = 2 and i < arrsize ('$skl_name')): jump 'rstloop_inner'
		i = 0
		x += 1
	if x < 3: jump 'rstloop_outer'
	killvar 'i'
	killvar 'x'
end

				elseif $npc_thdick[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 'well proportioned':
					npc_girth[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 11
				elseif $npc_thdick[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 'thicker than average':
					npc_girth[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 12
				elseif $npc_thdick[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 'thick':

				elseif $npc_thdick[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 'massive':

					su_temp_build_type = 1
					$npc_height_desc[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 'short'
				elseif npc_height[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] <= saveupdaterVars['av_height'] + 5:
					su_temp_build_type = 2
					$npc_height_desc[$saveupdaterVars['npcID']] = 'average'

					elseif saveupdaterVars['bmi'] < 250:

			elseif postjobQW = 999:

		if $job_status['pav_mailgirl'] = 'preemployed':
			!! v2 used preemployed + job_rank: 1=declined, 2=trial accepted, 3=job offered
			job_hiring_step['pav_mailgirl'] = job_rank['pav_mailgirl']
			$job_status['pav_mailgirl'] = ''
			job_rank['pav_mailgirl'] = 0
		elseif $job_status['pav_mailgirl'] = 'terminated':
			!! v4 early save: terminated was used for "skip Saturday" (no rank overload)
			!! Migrate to employed + suspended
			gs 'jobs', 'get_job_definition', 'pav_mailgirl'
			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'pav_mailgirl'
			gs 'jobs', 'suspend_job', 'pav_mailgirl'
		elseif $job_status['pav_mailgirl'] = 'employed' and job_rank['pav_mailgirl'] = 4:
			!! v2 used employed + rank 4 — re-employ cleanly via v4
			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'pav_mailgirl'
		end
	end

	!! city_diner_dishwasher + city_diner_floor_washer: Migrate from BurgerQW → v4
	if calendar_temp_new_flag['city_diner_dishwasher_v4'] = 0:
		calendar_temp_new_flag['city_diner_dishwasher_v4'] = 1

		!! Step 1: Employ dishwasher if player was ever hired (WashDishesCount >= 1)
		if BurgerQW['WashDishesCount'] >= 1 and $job_status['city_diner_dishwasher'] = '':
			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'city_diner_dishwasher'
		end

		!! Step 2: Employ floor washer if it was unlocked (WashFloorCount >= 1)
		if BurgerQW['WashFloorCount'] >= 1 and $job_status['city_diner_floor_washer'] = '':
			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'city_diner_floor_washer'
		end

		!! Step 3: Migrate WashDishesCount to job_shifts_total
		!! Split: attribute all shifts to dishwasher (we can''t distinguish which were floor)
		if BurgerQW['WashDishesCount'] > 1:
			job_shifts_total['city_diner_dishwasher'] = BurgerQW['WashDishesCount'] - 1
		end

		!! Step 4: Fire both jobs if work is permanently blocked (theft quest)
		if BurgerQW['WorkBlocked'] = 1:
			if $job_status['city_diner_dishwasher'] = 'employed': gs 'jobs', 'set_fired', 'city_diner_dishwasher'
			if $job_status['city_diner_floor_washer'] = 'employed': gs 'jobs', 'set_fired', 'city_diner_floor_washer'
		end

		!! Step 5: Clear deprecated BurgerQW variables (v4 handles these now)
		BurgerQW['WashDishesCount'] = 0
		BurgerQW['WashFloorCount'] = 0
		BurgerQW['WashedDishesLastDay'] = 0
		BurgerQW['WashFloorLastDay'] = 0
		BurgerQW['WorkBlocked'] = 0
	end

	!! city_diner_secretary: Migrate from BurgerQW['Secretary'] / SecretaryTimesWorked → v4

		calendar_temp_new_flag['city_diner_secretary_v4'] = 1

			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'city_diner_secretary'
			!! Preserve accumulated shift count for next monthly pay
			job_worked_count['city_diner_secretary'] = BurgerQW['SecretaryTimesWorked']
		end

		if BurgerQW['Secretary'] = 2 and $job_status['city_diner_secretary'] = '':
			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'city_diner_secretary'
			gs 'jobs', 'set_fired', 'city_diner_secretary'
		end

		BurgerQW['Secretary'] = 0
		BurgerQW['SecretaryTimesWorked'] = 0
		killvar 'paydaybistrosec'
		killvar 'kosiakBU'
	end

			job_shifts_total['city_cafe_waitress'] = smenaKafe
		end

		!! Step 2: Terminated in old system (-1)
		if workKafe['job'] = -1 and $job_status['city_cafe_waitress'] = '':
			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'city_cafe_waitress'
			gs 'jobs', 'set_terminated', 'city_cafe_waitress'
		end

		!! Step 3: Clear deprecated variables (v4 handles these now)
		workKafe['job'] = 0
		workKafe['at_work'] = 0
		killvar 'smenaKafe'
		killvar 'paydayKafe'
	end

	!! city_office_secretary: Migrate from workSec / sekDay / paydaySec / jobsec → v4
	if calendar_temp_new_flag['city_office_secretary_v4'] = 0:
		calendar_temp_new_flag['city_office_secretary_v4'] = 1

		if workSec = 1 and $job_status['city_office_secretary'] = '':
			gs 'jobs', 'set_employed', 'city_office_secretary'

				job_worked_count['city_office_secretary'] = sekDay
				job_shifts_total['city_office_secretary'] = sekDay
			end

		killvar 'sekDay'
		killvar 'paydaySec'

		calendar_temp_new_flag['pav_factory_v4'] = 1

			!! smenaFabrika * 600₽ = total accrued pay
			!! v4: paycheck = worked_count / 60 * 150₽, so worked_count = smenaFabrika * 600 / 150 * 60 = smenaFabrika * 240
			job_worked_count['pav_factory'] = smenaFabrika * 240
		end

		killvar 'smenaFabrika'

		killvar 'FabrikaProgulDay'

	if calendar_temp_new_flag['city_pussycats_clerk_v4'] = 0:
		calendar_temp_new_flag['city_pussycats_clerk_v4'] = 1

				job_missed_total['city_pussycats_clerk'] = young_shop_miss
				job_miss_acknowledged['city_pussycats_clerk'] = young_shop_warned

				end
				!! Check old slot 2 (should be slot 1)

				if $temp_su_data <> '' and $job_booking['city_strip_stripper, ' + $str(temp_su_day) + ', 1'] = '':
					!! Move from slot 2 to slot 1
					gs 'jobs', 'cancel_booking', 'city_strip_stripper', temp_su_day, 2
					gs 'jobs', 'book_slot', 'city_strip_stripper', temp_su_day, 1, $temp_su_data
				end
				temp_su_day += 1
				jump 'strip_sched_migrate_loop'
			end
			killvar 'temp_su_day'
			killvar 'temp_su_data'
		end

		!! Clear legacy schedule variables
		temp_su_i = 1
		:strip_sched_clear_loop
		if temp_su_i <= 7:
			if strip_club[$str(temp_su_i) + '_shift_1'] <> 0:
				gs 'jobs', 'book_slot', 'city_strip_stripper', daystart - week + temp_su_i, 0, '旧版班次'
			end
			if strip_club[$str(temp_su_i) + '_shift_2'] <> 0:

			end

			strip_club[$str(temp_su_i) + '_shift_2_taken'] = 0
			if strip_club['next_' + $str(temp_su_i) + '_shift_1'] <> 0:

			end

			strip_club['next_' + $str(temp_su_i) + '_shift_2_taken'] = 0

		strip_club['shifts_required'] = 0

				gs 'jobs', 'set_fired', 'city_pornstudio_actress'
			else

			end

			if prodcosttrue > 0:
				job_booking_debt['city_pornstudio_actress'] = prodcosttrue
			end

			if pornstack > 0:
				job_bookings_active['city_pornstudio_actress'] = pornstack
			end

			!! Migrate existing pfilmday/porntaken entries to v4 booking records

						$job_booking['city_pornstudio_actress, ' + $str(temp_psa_ds) + ', 0'] = $str(pfilmday[temp_psa_i])
					end
				end
				temp_psa_i += 1
				jump 'saveupdater_porn_booking_loop'
			end
			killvar 'temp_psa_i'
			killvar 'temp_psa_ds'

			!! Migrate film count as lifetime shifts
			if film > 0:

			end
		end
	end

	!! Rename pornstudio_actress → city_pornstudio_actress for saves that ran the old migrator
	if calendar_temp_new_flag['city_pornstudio_actress_rename'] = 0:
		calendar_temp_new_flag['city_pornstudio_actress_rename'] = 1

		if $job_status['pornstudio_actress'] <> '':

			$job_status['city_pornstudio_actress'] = $job_status['pornstudio_actress']
			job_rank['city_pornstudio_actress'] = job_rank['pornstudio_actress']
			job_added['city_pornstudio_actress'] = job_added['pornstudio_actress']
			job_termination_day['city_pornstudio_actress'] = job_termination_day['pornstudio_actress']
			$job_termination_reason['city_pornstudio_actress'] = $job_termination_reason['pornstudio_actress']
			job_pay_rate['city_pornstudio_actress'] = job_pay_rate['pornstudio_actress']
			$job_pay_interval['city_pornstudio_actress'] = $job_pay_interval['pornstudio_actress']

	!! Fix porn booking slot keys missing the slot index from the broken pornstudio_actress_v4 migration.

	!! Correct format: '$job_booking[''city_pornstudio_actress, daystart, 0'']'

	!! Organized post variables
	if postamountrand <> 0:		post_vars['load']	= postamountrand	& killvar 'postamountrand'
	if posteventcounter <> 0:	post_vars['round']	= posteventcounter	& killvar 'posteventcounter'
	if posteventQW1 <> 0:		post_vars['qw_1']	= posteventQW1		& killvar 'posteventQW1'
	if posteventQW3 <> 0:		post_vars['qw_3']	= posteventQW3		& killvar 'posteventQW3'
	if posteventQW2 <> 0:		post_vars['qw_2']	= posteventQW2		& killvar 'posteventQW2'
	if posteventQW4 <> 0:		post_vars['qw_4']	= posteventQW4		& killvar 'posteventQW4'
	if posteventQW5 <> 0:		post_vars['qw_5']	= posteventQW5		& killvar 'posteventQW5'
	if poststreets <> 0:		post_vars['danger'] = poststreets		& killvar 'poststreets'
	if postbandQW <> 0:			post_vars['bandit']	= postbandQW		& killvar 'postbandQW'
	if postexhibi <> 0:			post_vars['exhib']	= postexhibi		& killvar 'postexhibi'
	if posthj <> 0:				post_vars['hj']		= posthj			& killvar 'posthj'
	if postbj <> 0:				post_vars['bj']		= postbj			& killvar 'postbj'
	if postlate <> 0:			post_vars['late']	= postlate			& killvar 'postlate'
	if postburn <> 0:			post_vars['burn']	= postburn			& killvar 'postburn'

	!! Consolidated icons, kill row 5 in case there are any leftovers
	killvar 'sd_icons_5'

	!! Update old "succubusQW" variable values to new temporary values. These will be updated again in the next update.
	if succubus['succubusQW'] = 3: succubus['succubusQW'] = 12
	if succubus['succubusQW'] = 4: succubus['succubusQW'] = 11
	if succubus['succubusQW'] = 5: succubus['succubusQW'] = 14

	!! Show feature update popups for 0.9.8 and 0.9.9
	if temp_saveupdater_guard['show_0.9.9.0_features'] = 0:
		temp_saveupdater_guard['show_0.9.9.0_features'] = 1
		update_report_last = 97
		update_report_new = 99
	end

	!! ── stat_display: migrate group membership to composite-keyed arrays ──
	$temp_mig_g[0] = 'mental'	& $temp_mig_g[1] = 'sport'		& $temp_mig_g[2] = 'combat'
	$temp_mig_g[3] = 'beauty'	& $temp_mig_g[4] = 'artistic'	& $temp_mig_g[5] = 'job'
	temp_mig_gi = 0
	:save0900_grpmig_skloop
	if temp_mig_gi < arrsize('$temp_mig_g'):
		$temp_mig_old = 'skill_grp_' + $temp_mig_g[temp_mig_gi]
		temp_mig_j = 0
		:save0900_grpmig_skmloop
		if temp_mig_j < arrsize('$' + $temp_mig_old):
			$temp_mig_val = $dyneval('$result = $<<$temp_mig_old>>[<<temp_mig_j>>]')
			dynamic '$skill_grp[''<<$temp_mig_g[temp_mig_gi]>>_<<temp_mig_j>>''] = ''<<$temp_mig_val>>'''
			temp_mig_j += 1
			jump 'save0900_grpmig_skmloop'
		end
		killvar $temp_mig_old
		temp_mig_gi += 1
		jump 'save0900_grpmig_skloop'
	end
	killvar 'temp_mig_g'

	$temp_mig_g[0] = 'family'	& $temp_mig_g[1] = 'coolkid'	& $temp_mig_g[2] = 'jock'
	$temp_mig_g[3] = 'nerd'		& $temp_mig_g[4] = 'gopnik'		& $temp_mig_g[5] = 'staff'
	$temp_mig_g[6] = 'pavlovsk'	& $temp_mig_g[7] = 'city'
	temp_mig_gi = 0
	:save0900_grpmig_rlloop
	if temp_mig_gi < arrsize('$temp_mig_g'):
		$temp_mig_old = 'rel_grp_' + $temp_mig_g[temp_mig_gi]
		temp_mig_j = 0
		:save0900_grpmig_rlmloop
		if temp_mig_j < arrsize('$' + $temp_mig_old):
			$temp_mig_val = $dyneval('$result = $<<$temp_mig_old>>[<<temp_mig_j>>]')
			dynamic '$rel_grp[''<<$temp_mig_g[temp_mig_gi]>>_<<temp_mig_j>>''] = ''<<$temp_mig_val>>'''
			temp_mig_j += 1
			jump 'save0900_grpmig_rlmloop'
		end
		killvar $temp_mig_old
		temp_mig_gi += 1
		jump 'save0900_grpmig_rlloop'
	end
	killvar 'temp_mig_g'
	killvar 'temp_mig_old'
	killvar 'temp_mig_val'
	killvar 'temp_mig_gi'
	killvar 'temp_mig_j'
end

			!!------------------VERSION 0.9.9.1--------------------!!

if temp_current_save_version < 00090901:
	!! Fix overlapping string and integer cells
	if arrsize('$accessible_property_codes') = 0:
		su0991_max = arrsize('accessible_property')
		su0991_i = 0
		if su0991_max > 0:
			:su0991_accessible_property_migration_loop
				$su0991_code = $accessible_property[su0991_i]
				if $su0991_code <> '' and $home_name[$su0991_code] <> '' and arrpos('$accessible_property_codes', $su0991_code) = -1:
					$accessible_property_codes[] = $su0991_code
				end
				su0991_i += 1
			if su0991_i < su0991_max: jump 'su0991_accessible_property_migration_loop'
		end
		killvar 'su0991_max'
		killvar 'su0991_i'
		killvar 'su0991_code'
	end

	!! Fix overlapping string and integer cells
	su0991_max = arrsize('$npc_index')
	su0991_i = 0
	if su0991_max > 0:
		:su0991_npc_label_migration_loop
			$su0991_npcID = $npc_index[su0991_i]
			if $npc_usedname[$su0991_npcID] <> '':
				if $npc_style_label[$su0991_npcID] = '':
					if npc_style[$su0991_npcID] = 1:
						$npc_style_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'normal'
					elseif npc_style[$su0991_npcID] = 2:
						$npc_style_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'gopnik'
					elseif npc_style[$su0991_npcID] = 3:
						$npc_style_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'nerdy'
					elseif npc_style[$su0991_npcID] = 4:
						$npc_style_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'sporty'
					elseif npc_style[$su0991_npcID] = 5:
						$npc_style_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'fashionable'
					else
						$npc_style_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'businessman'
					end
				end

				if $npc_apt_type_label[$su0991_npcID] = '':
					if npc_apt_type[$su0991_npcID] = 6:
						$npc_apt_type_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'mansion'
					elseif npc_apt_type[$su0991_npcID] = 4 or npc_apt_type[$su0991_npcID] = 5:
						$npc_apt_type_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'multi_bedroom'
					elseif npc_apt_type[$su0991_npcID] = 3:
						$npc_apt_type_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'one_bedroom'
					elseif npc_apt_type[$su0991_npcID] = 2:
						$npc_apt_type_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'studio'
					elseif npc_apt_type[$su0991_npcID] = 1:
						$npc_apt_type_label[$su0991_npcID] = 'student_dorm'
					end
				end

				if $npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = '':
					if npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] <= 4:
						npc_fidelity_num[$su0991_npcID] = npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID]
						$npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = 'cheater'
					elseif npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] <= 6:
						$npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = 'seduced'
					elseif npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = 7:
						$npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = 'open'
					elseif npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] > 0:
						$npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = 'faithful'
					end
					npc_fidelity_num[$su0991_npcID] = npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID]
					npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = 0
				elseif $npc_fidelity_label[$su0991_npcID] <> '':
					$npc_fidelity[$su0991_npcID] = $npc_fidelity_label[$su0991_npcID]
					$npc_fidelity_label[$su0991_npcID] = ''
				end
			end
			su0991_i += 1
		if su0991_i < su0991_max: jump 'su0991_npc_label_migration_loop'
	end
	killvar 'su0991_max'
	killvar 'su0991_i'
	killvar 'su0991_npcID'

	gs 'archetypes', 'init'

	if stat['last_sex_day_strap'] > stat['last_sex_day_vaginal']:
		stat['last_sex_day_vaginal'] = stat['last_sex_day_strap']
	end
	if stat['last_sex_day_strap'] > stat['last_sex_day']:
		stat['last_sex_day'] = stat['last_sex_day_strap']
	end
	gs 'array', 'remove_element', 'stat', 'last_sex_day_strap'

	!! last_rape_day was folded into the new last_sex_day_rape family;
	!! carry its historical value forward before dropping it.
	if stat['last_rape_day'] > stat['last_sex_day_rape']:
		stat['last_sex_day_rape'] = stat['last_rape_day']
	end
	if stat['last_rape_day'] > stat['last_sex_day_rape_known']:
		stat['last_sex_day_rape_known'] = stat['last_rape_day']
	end
	if stat['last_rape_day'] > stat['last_sex_day']:
		stat['last_sex_day'] = stat['last_rape_day']
	end
	gs 'array', 'remove_element', 'stat', 'last_rape_day'
	gs 'archetypes', 'init'

	!! Migrate anal virginity stats to proper handler
	if $virgin_stats['lost_cause_anal'] = '':
		if stat['anal'] > 0 or stat['anal_fist'] > 0 or stat['hidden_anal'] > 0:
			$virgin_stats['lost_cause_anal'] = 'anal'
		elseif stat['anal_dildo'] > 0:
			$virgin_stats['lost_cause_anal'] = 'dildo'
		elseif stat['anal_strap'] > 0:
			$virgin_stats['lost_cause_anal'] = 'strapon'
		elseif pcs_ass > 0:
			$virgin_stats['lost_cause_anal'] = 'unknown'
		end
	end
end
